So the vanilla mechanism for water horizontal slowdown is:

1. `velocity` itself is NOT reduced by water. Running in water still reaches vx=3 (maxRunSpeed), acceleration 0.08, etc.
2. But at position integration time (Player.Update :27882-27884), when `wet && !merman && !ignoreWater && !trident`, the player calls `WetCollision(fallThrough, flag29, 0.5f)` — moving position by only `velocity * 0.5` horizontally (vertical kept full except collision-adjusted). Honey = 0.25, shimmerWet = 0.375? no wait: shimmerWet uses num111=0.375, honeyWet uses movementSpeed=0.25, wet/lavaWet uses num109=0.5.

So effective horizontal displacement in water = velocity × 0.5, but velocity stays 3 → and the dust gate `velocity.X > num` where `num = (accRunSpeed + maxRunSpeed)/2` — with bare feet: accRunSpeed = 3, maxRunSpeed = 3 → num = 3. Wait, but the ramp-up branch requires velocity.X < accRunSpeed (=3), and dust triggers when velocity.X > num = 3. Both conditions: vx < 3 (branch entry) AND vx > 3 (dust) → mutually exclusive → dust never triggers barefoot, in water or not!

Hold on, but wait: in vanilla, does `wet` affect maxRunSpeed at all? From the greps: no. maxRunSpeed stays 3 barefoot in water. accRunSpeed = maxRunSpeed = 3 at reset (:24099), only overwritten by boots (6) / lightning frostspark (6.75) etc.

So in vanilla:
- Barefoot: accRunSpeed == maxRunSpeed == 3 → num = 3 → ramp branch requires vx < 3, dust requires vx > 3 → never both → no dust, ever, on land or in water. 
- Hermes boots: accRunSpeed = 6, maxRunSpeed = 3 → num = 4.5 → ramp branch vx < 6, dust when vx > 4.5 → dust in ramp zone (4.5, 6). In water? vx still ramps the same (no water reduction of run params!) — BUT actually wait, can you even ramp to 4.5 in water? The acceleration chain is unchanged in water, so yes: running in shallow water on the ground with Hermes boots still ramps vx to ~6 (with sawtooth around maxRunSpeed... wait no — ramp goes above maxRunSpeed because the second branch accelerates ×0.2 until accRunSpeed). So with Hermes boots in water, vanilla still spawns fast-run dust (since vx isn't reduced by water; only position integration is ×0.5).

Hmm wait, actually, but hold on — is that right? Let me double check the ramp branch condition in water. In HorizontalMovement, the branches are as read above. There's no wet gate. So yes, with boots in water, vx ramps to 6 and SpawnFastRunParticles triggers (velocity.X > num=4.5, velocity.Y == 0 — you need to be on the ground underwater, vy==0).

Actually wait — velocity.Y == 0 underwater on ground: when standing on the lake bottom, gravity applies each frame but collision zeroes vy → vy == 0 mostly each frame (in vanilla, when standing on ground, TileCollision zeroes velocity.Y). So yes.

So the vanilla truth:
1. Water does NOT reduce maxRunSpeed/accRunSpeed/runAcceleration/runSlowdown at all (Player.cs:24096-24099 reset; multiplications at 25694-25695 moveSpeed, 26184-26300 equipment chain — none wet-gated).
2. Water slowdown happens at collision/integration: Player.cs:27872-27885 — shimmerWet→WetCollision(0.375), honeyWet→WetCollision(0.25), wet&&!merman&&!ignoreWater&&!trident→WetCollision(0.5) (lavaWet also 0.5; both num109/num110 are 0.5). Position advances velocity×0.5 horizontally; velocity is unchanged (except by tile collision).
3. Dust gate: num = (accRunSpeed+maxRunSpeed)/2 computed at :19490; ramp branch :19705 (controlRight && vx<accRunSpeed...) with dust at :19730 (vx>num && vy==0 && !mount.Active).

So the bug in our repo: we model water slowdown as maxRun ×= 0.55 and runAcc ×= 0.6, which (a) doesn't match vanilla (should be position-integration ×0.5), and (b) creates the accRun > maxRun band for barefoot (since accRun isn't scaled), triggering dust.

Now — how is our repo's physics structured? Does our Player.ts integrate position as pos += vx directly somewhere with a water multiplier? Let me check. ## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-17T04:50:29.141Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Now I see the structure. Our repo currently:
1. In the Run section (:1740): `maxRun *= inWater && !swimFree ? 0.55 : 1` — a self-invented model.
2. In runAcc (:1765): `* (this.inWater ? 0.6 : 1)`.
3. Movement integration: `moveAndCollide(this, world, this.vx, this.vy)` — no water factor.

Vanilla truth:
1. Run params NOT affected by water at all.
2. Water slows position integration: WetCollision(fallThrough, flag29, movementSpeed) with movementSpeed = 0.5 for water (num109/num110 both 0.5), 0.25 for honey, 0.375 for shimmer (num111). And gates: shimmerWet → 0.375; honeyWet && !ignoreWater → 0.25; wet && !merman && !ignoreWater && !trident → 0.5 (lavaWet also 0.5); else DryCollision.
3. In WetCollision (Player.cs:23157-23172): 
   - `velocity = TileCollision(position, velocity, ...)` — full velocity is passed to TileCollision (so collision resolution operates on full velocity; onGround detection normal).
   - Then `vector2 = velocity * movementSpeed` — but if velocity.X changed due to collision (hit a wall), vector2.X = velocity.X (full, un-scaled); same for Y.
   - position += vector2.

So effectively: horizontal movement per tick in water = vx * 0.5 unless blocked by wall (then the clamped velocity.X is applied full — but the clamped value is already small since it's the remaining distance to the wall, typically less than the movement anyway; this detail matters only at walls).

Interesting: velocity itself unchanged by water; vx can reach 3 (or 6 with boots ramp). The dust gate with boots in water: vx > (6+3)/2=4.5 && vy==0 → dust spawns even underwater in vanilla. Barefoot: num=(3+3)/2=3; branch entry vx<accRunSpeed=3, dust requires vx>3 → never. 

So vanilla semantics: barefoot never dust (land or water); Hermes boots in water DO produce run dust (assuming vy==0, standing on bottom). Let me double check SpawnFastRunParticles to see if there is any wet gate inside.

Also Item_17 footsteps — that's in SpawnFastRunParticles itself (:35517 area). Let me read SpawnFastRunParticles.
```

</details>

  /** 远端代理移动子集物理（与 Player.fixedUpdate 同参数：加速/摩擦/跳跃/重力/碰撞/动画） */
  private stepRemoteProxy(p: Player) {
    const st = this.world.store;
    // 液体粗判（游泳减速/上浮需要；同 Player 采样：脚底上方 4px）
    p.inWater = st.liquid[st.idx(Math.floor(p.cx / TILE), Math.floor((p.y + p.h - 4) / TILE))] > 100;
    const ix = p.inputX;
    if (ix !== 0) {
      p.vx += ix * PLAYER_WALK_ACCEL * (p.inWater ? 0.6 : 1);
      p.facing = ix;
    } else {
      p.vx *= p.onGround ? PLAYER_FRICTION : PLAYER_AIR_FRICTION;
      if (Math.abs(p.vx) < 0.05) p.vx = 0;
    }
    const maxSpd = PLAYER_WALK_MAX * (p.inWater ? 0.55 : 1);
    p.vx = Math.max(-maxSpd, Math.min(maxSpd, p.vx));
    if (p.inWater) {
      // 水物理（:24150-24155）：gravity 0.2 / maxFall 5（与主线程 Player 同步对齐）
      if (p.inputJump) p.vy = Math.max(p.vy - 0.62, -4.4);
      p.vy += GRAVITY * 0.5;
      p.vy = Math.max(-6.01, Math.min(5.0, p.vy));
    } else {
      // 原版跳模型（:20384-20396）：恒钉平台段 + vy==0 终止（代理无装备链，裸常量）
      if (p.inputJump && p.onGround) { p.vy = -PLAYER_JUMP_SPEED; p.jumpHold = PLAYER_JUMP_TICKS; }
      if (p.jumpHold > 0) {
        if (p.vy === 0) p.jumpHold = 0;
        else if (p.inputJump) { p.vy = -PLAYER_JUMP_SPEED; p.jumpHold--; }
      }
      p.vy = Math.min(p.vy + GRAVITY, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
    }
    // 松键 +0.01（:22446 原版语义；曾 -2 截断）
    if (!p.inputJump && p.vy < 0) p.vy += 0.01;

window.__swItems[hermesKey] : -1;\n  // Item_17 脚步声计数（spawnFastRunParticles → playSfxFiles）\n  let stepSounds = 0;\n  const origPlay = g.playSfxFiles;\n  if (origPlay) g.playSfxFiles = (files, ...a) => { if (files?.includes('Item_17')) stepSounds++; return origPlay(files, ...a); };\n  // 平地跑道 + 可开关的 1 格深水层（feet 行灌满 255/水，水头在脚、头露水面）\n  const bx = Math.floor(p.x / 16), by = Math.floor(p.y / 16) + 6;\n  const under = st.type[st.idx(bx, by + 2)];\n  for (let dx = -120; dx <= 120; dx++) for (let dy = -14; dy <= 2; dy++)\n    st.setTile(bx + dx, by + dy, dy === 2 ? -1;\n  // 物品 id 只能走 window.__swItems（g.itemId 查 vi_ 键会 undefined）\n  const hermesKey = Object.keys(window.__swItems).find(k => /^vi_54_/.test(k));\n  const hermesId = hermesKey ? window.__swItems[hermesKey] : -1;\n  // Item_17 脚步声计数（spawnFastRunParticles → playSfxFiles）\n  let stepSounds = 0;\n  const origPlay = g.playSfxFiles;\n  if (origPlay) g.playSfxFiles = (files, ...a) => { if (files?.includes('Item_17')) stepSounds++; return origPlay(files, ...a); };\n  // 平地跑道（±120 净空）+ 两端 3 格厚坝墙（±110 起）+ 可开关的 1 格深水层（feet 行灌满，\n  // 头露水面）。坝墙防液体模拟外溢扩散（首轮探针瘫痪根因）。\n  const bx = Math.floor(p.x / 16), by = Math.floor(p.y / 16) + 6;\n  const under = st.type[st.idx(bx, by + 2)];\n  for (let dx = -120; dx <= 120; dx++) for (let dy = -14; dy <= 2; dy++)\n    st.setTile(bx + dx, by + dy, dy === 2 ? under : 0);\n  for (const wx of [-113, -112, -111, 110, 111, 112])\n    for (let dy = -14; dy <= 1; dy++) st.setTile(bx + wx, by + dy, under);\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 300));\n  const setWater = (on) => {\n    for (let dx = -110; dx <= 109; dx++) st.setLiquid(bx + dx, by + 1, on ? 255 : 0, 1);\n  };\n  const settle = async () => {\n    p.x = bx * 16 + 8; p.y = (by + 2) * 16 - p.h; p.vx = 0; p.vy = 0; p.onGround = false;\n    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 400));\n  };\n  const runFor = async (ms) => {\n    const before = g.vanillaDust.pool.filter(d => d.type === 16).length;\n    const soundsBefore = stepSounds, t0 = tick();\n    let vxMax = 0;\n    g.input.touchKeys.add('KeyD');\n    const tm = performance.now();\n    while (performance.now() - tm < ms) { vxMax = Math.max(vxMax, Math.abs(p.vx)); await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 50)); }\n    g.input.touchKeys.delete('KeyD');\n    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 150));\n    const tps = t0 >= 0 && tick() >= 0 ? tick() - t0 : -1;\n    return {\n      dust16: g.vanillaDust.pool.filter(d => d.type === 16).length - before,\n      steps: stepSounds - soundsBefore,\n      vxMax: +vxMax.toFixed(2), ticks: tps,\n      inWater: p.inWater, liqFactor: p.liqFactor, onGround: p.onGround,\n      accRun: p.equipStats.accRunSpeed ?? 0,\n    };\n  };\n  const res = { hermesKey, hermesId };\n  // ① 裸装·水中\n  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) p.inv.armor[i] = null;\n  await settle(); setWater(true); await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 400)); await settle();\n  res.barefootWater = await runFor(3000);\n  // ② 裸装·岸上（抽干水）\n  setWater(false); await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 400)); await settle();\n  res.barefootLand = await runFor(3000);\n  // ③ 赫尔墨斯·水中（armor[3] 功能配饰槽）——8s 保证爬坡过 4.5 阈值后有余量\n  p.inv.armor[3] = hermesId >= 0 ? { id: hermesId, stack: 1 } : null;\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 500));\n  setWater(true); await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 400)); await settle();\n  res.hermesWater = await runFor(8000);\n  // ④ 对照：赫尔墨斯·岸上（同时长——尘量级应与水中同档=水中无抑制）\n  setWater(false); await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 400)); await settle();\n  res.hermesLand = await runFor(8000);\n  if (origPlay) g.playSfxFiles = origPlay;\n  return res;\n});\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify(out, null, 1));\nawait browser.close();\n"

The `!paused` at 4610 only gates animation rebaking — cosmetic, irrelevant to dust/sound/vx. So the plan works:

- set `g.paused = true` (rAF loop stops calling fixedUpdate)
- drive ticks manually: `for (let i=0;i<N;i++) g['fixedUpdate'](1/60)`
- input: touchKeys KeyD (read inside fixedUpdate via input layer — the player inputX is injected... let me make sure the player's inputX updates from touchKeys during fixedUpdate. The _runfxgate probe used touchKeys with the natural loop — the injection happens somewhere per tick. Since I call the same fixedUpdate, same injection path runs. ✓)

But wait — does `g['fixedUpdate']` exist at runtime? `private fixedUpdate(dt: number)` — TS private, still a normal method at runtime. ✓

One risk: double-stepping if I forget to pause, or the loop's `if (!this.paused)` also gates `this.acc += elapsed` — with paused=true the accumulator stays 0 — good, no double steps.

Also `windSway.active` — cosmetic.

Now the phases become fully deterministic:
- Phase runs: hold KeyD, call fixedUpdate × 300 (5s of game time), release, settle ticks × 30.
- Dust/sounds counted synchronously after.
- No wall-clock dependence, immune to machine load. 

Timing concerns: each fixedUpdate call is synchronous; 300 calls per phase × 4 phases + settles ≈ 1400 calls — fast (game runs 60/s normally; each tick ~2-5ms → 300 ticks ≈ 1s wall time per phase; total ~5-8s). 

Also settle(): teleport player; then run ~30 ticks manually to let collision settle + equip refresh. equipStats refresh — when does it recompute? Probably each fixedUpdate or on armor change. 500ms wait before was for that; with manual ticks, run 30 ticks after equipping boots.

Water set/drain: setLiquid fires listeners; liquid sim runs inside fixedUpdate (addWater queue). With manual ticks, the water settles within ticks. Do ~30 ticks after pouring.

Rewrite the probe with manual stepping. Keep the dams (still good hygiene — water flow runs during ticks). Keep tick counts fixed: barefoot 200 ticks, hermes 400 ticks (crossing 4.5 at ~tick 129 → 270 dust ticks).

Also keep recording tps... not needed now — ticks are exact. But record wallMs per phase for info.

One more: the spawnFastRunParticles dust gate ALSO needs vy===0 — with manual stepping all same.

And `requestAnimationFrame` no longer needed. Also remove the fps hack. Update header comment.

## 修复报告：裸装水中跑尘+脚步声

### 一、原版水中跑动真值（Terarria1456/Terraria/Player.cs，先证后修）

**原版 Run 参数完全不受水影响**。全链核查无任何 wet 因子：
- 每帧复位 `Player.cs:24096-24099`：`maxRunSpeed=3 / runAcceleration=0.08 / runSlowdown=0.2 / accRunSpeed=maxRunSpeed`——无水乘区；
- 唯一通用乘区 `:25694-25695`（`runAcceleration/maxRunSpeed *= moveSpeed`）；装备链 `:26184-26300`（empress/magilumen/shadow/dune/冰面/坐骑等）grep 全量确认**无一条 wet 门**；
- `wet` 只改重力/终端/跳跃（`:24129-24159`：水 0.2/5.01、蜜 0.1/3、merman 0.3/7、三叉戟 0.25/6）。

**原版水中减速全在位移积分层**（`Player.cs:27858-27888`）：按液体选档 `shimmerWet→0.375(num111) > honeyWet&&!ignoreWater→0.25 > wet&&!merman&&!ignoreWater&&!trident→0.5（岩浆同 0.5，num109/num110）> 其余 DryCollision`；`WetCollision(:23157-23172)` 把**原值速度**送进 TileCollision，位移 `position += velocity×factor`——即水中 vx 照常加速到 3，只是每 tick 只走一半路程。`ignoreWater` 来源=游泳族坐骑 buff（:9585-9604）∪ 脚蹼药 109（:9851-9852）∪ 星旋强化翼 wingSlot26（:14906-14908）。

**尘门原版无水门**：调用点 `:19700/:19730`（`vx>num=(accRunSpeed+maxRunSpeed)/2 && vy==0 && !mount.Active`）与 `SpawnFastRunParticles:19935` 内部均无 wet 检查——**裸装因互斥带 [3,3) 空永不触发（水陆皆然）；穿靴水中照冒尘照响 Item_17**。

结论：旧代码 `maxRun×0.55 / runAcc×0.6` 是自造速度模型，假设成立（accRun 不缩 → 裸装水中带 [1.65,3) 非空 → 尘误触发）；正确修法不是给 accRun 也乘系数，而是**把水减速整体搬到位移层**。

### 二、修改内容

1. **`game/src/entities/Player.ts`**（3 处）：
   - Run 段摘除 `maxRun ×(inWater?0.55:1)`（原 :1740）与 `runAcc ×(inWater?0.6:1)`（原 :1765）；
   - 积分前（moveAndCollide 之前）新增 `liqFactor` 选档段，1:1 复刻 `:27874-27888`（shimmer 0.375 / 蜜 0.25 / 水·岩浆 0.5，`ignoreWater = ridingMountSwimFree ∪ 脚蹼药 ∪ wing.slot===26`，merman/手持三叉戟 277 豁免），经 `moveAndCollide` 既有 `liqFactor` 入口缩位移（NPC MoveWhileWet 同机制，velocity 不动）；
   - 同步修正 :1922 过时注释。
2. **`game/src/core/Game.ts` `stepRemoteProxy`**：远端代理同步换模型——摘除 0.55/0.6 速度因子，改 `p.liqFactor = inWater?0.5:1` 位移缩放，与主线程保持“同参数”承诺。

### 三、探针数字（scripts/_waterbare.mjs，tick 计数驱动，5201 私有实例）

| 组 | ticks | 尘16 | Item_17 | vxMax | liqFactor |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ① 裸装·水中 | 200 | **0** | **0** | 3.04 | 0.5 |
| ② 裸装·岸上 | 202 | **0** | **0** | 3.04 | 1 |
| ③ 赫尔墨斯·水中 | 360 | **220** | **26** | 6.01 | 0.5 |
| ④ 赫尔墨斯·岸上（对照） | 361 | 221 | 26 | 6.01 | 1 |

③=④ 量级完全一致（水中零抑制）、vx 两态同为 6.01（速度不被水缩、只缩位移）——与原版 WetCollision 架构逐项吻合；220≈(360−130 越阈 tick)、26≈冷却 9t，算术自洽。

### 四、验证与遗留说明

- `npx tsc --noEmit`：Player.ts / Game.ts 零错；`vitest player-run-parity + water-jump` 10/10 绿。
- 排障过程发现两处与本题无关的事实，未改动仅报备：a) `Game.playSfxFiles` 是依赖 `this` 的方法——探针包装必须 `.bind(g)`，未绑定转发会在**首个 playSfxFiles 调用**（恰为靴族首声 Item_17）抛 TypeError 炸死 rAF 主循环，症状酷似“游戏停摆”（参考脚本 `_runfxgate.mjs:23` 也有同款隐患）；b) 中途一次 `[worldGen worker] rng is not defined` 来自并行会话当时正在改的 CaveHousePass.ts，已自行恢复。二分用临时探针已删（删前 pgrep 确认无关联进程），`_waterbare.mjs` 留作回归探针。
